Another algorithmic idea: Use second-order Newton on active set. Since uniform is second-order minimizable with antisymmetric perturbations. We can explicitly add a second-order correction to uniform: p = uniform + ε R, where R chosen to minimize increase of all c (or maximize drop of current max) to second order. Let's build the optimizer generic:
```
def logsum_p_suggestions(n, rng, count): ...

def evaluate_fft(p):
    c=fast_conv(p)
    return 2*n*max(c)

def fast_conv(p):
    n=p.size; M=1<<((2*n-2).bit_length()) # at least 2n-1
    A=rfft(p,M); c=irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]; return c

def correlation_weight_p(w,p):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1; M=1<<((3*n-3).bit_length()) # >=3n-3? Actually L+n-1=3n-2? bit length with -3? For 3n-2. Use (3*n-2)-1? Let's do size=3*n-2; M=1<<((size-1).bit_length())
    pr=p[::-1]
    Fw=rfft(w,M); Fp=rfft(pr,M)
    corr=irfft(Fw*Fp,M)
    return corr[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need M size: conv length n+L-1 = n+2n-1-1=3n-2. To get indices n-1 through 2n-2 inclusive length n. M >=3n-2. `1 << ((3*n-2 - 1).bit_length())`? For size=3, if bit_length(2)=2 ->4; okay. Use `M = 1 << (len_target - 1).bit_length()`.

Gradient:
```
def logsum_grad_weights(p, lam):
    c=conv(p)
    cmax=np.max(c)
    x=lam*(c - cmax)
    # c - cmax <=0; min x maybe -lam * cmax (if c= -) ~ -0.75; safe. But if all zero? no.
    w=np.exp(x); w/=w.sum()
    h=corr(w,p)
    dz=p*(h - h.mean())
    return dz, c, cmax
```
If lam very small, x~0, w flat.
True D =2n*cmax.

```
def make_shape(n, rng, mode):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    x=i/n
    raw = np.ones(n)
    # edge alpha random
    alL = rng.uniform(0.2,0.55); alR=...
    AL = rng.uniform(0.0,1.5); AR=...
    if AL>0: raw += AL * n**alL / (i+0.5)**alL
    if AR>0: raw += AR * n**alR / (n-i-0.5)**alR
    # Fourier random:
    max_amp = rng.uniform(0.1,0.8)
    for k in 1..6:
        amp = max_amp * rng.uniform(0.0,1.0) / k? 
        phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
        raw += amp * np.sin(2π*k*i/n + phase)
    raw = np.clip(raw, 1e-3, None)
    p=raw/raw.sum()
    return p
```

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_logsum(z0, time_end, rng, initial_lr=0.05, best_p=None):
    z=z0.copy(); p=softmax(z); m/v zeros; t=0; lr=initial_lr; lam=5.0; best_score=...
    no_improve_iters=0
    while time < time_end:
       dz, c, cmax = grad(p, lam)
       score=2n*cmax
       if score<best: update
       no_improve += 1
       if no_improve > patience_iters:
           # reset lam maybe cycle, perturb z from best_z
           z = best_z + perturbation(scale)
           m=v=0; t=0; lr = ...; lam = random in [5,20,50]?
           no_improve=0; patience=max(100, patience//2)
       # schedule lam: maybe increase gradually every 2000 steps: lam = min(max_lam, lam*1.01)
       # Or phase based on time: first 30% lam 5, next 30% 10, next 30% 30, final 50.
       t+=1; z=adam; p=softmax(z); clip z.
    return best_score, best_z, best_p
```

Now, how to resize parent `height_sequence_1`. We need handle global variable possibly. Code:
```
def resize_sequence(arr, n_new):
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    a=a/s
    n_old=a.size
    if n_old==n_new: return a
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n_old)
    y_old=a / n_old # density approx
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
    f_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, y_old)
    p_new=f_new * n_new
    p_new=np.clip(p_new,0,None)
    ps=p_new.sum()
    if ps<=0: return None
    p_new/=ps
    return p_new
```
Using linear interpolation of density. Could add random.

Generate shape candidates using rng. Need ensure reproducibility: use seed maybe 12345. Could use parent.

Let's define `build_basis(n, rng)`:
```
idx = np.arange(n); x = idx/n
basis=[]
# edge alpha
for a in [0.3,0.4,0.45]:
    basis.append(centered( n**a/(idx+0.5)**a ))
    basis.append(centered( n**a/(n-idx-0.5)**a ))
# polynomial
for power in [1,2,3]: basis.append(centered((2*x-1)**power))
# trig
for k in [1..6]:
    basis.append(centered(np.sin(2πk x)))
    basis.append(centered(np.cos(2πk x)))
# maybe edge*poly
for a in [0.4]:
    basis.append(centered(left_edge * x))
    basis.append(centered(right_edge*(1-x)))
```

`optimize_theta_slice` details:
```
def optimize_theta_slice(Phi, theta0, time_end, rng, n, lam_init, max_lam, lr, best_p_global, best_score_global):
    theta=theta0.copy()
    logits=theta@Phi
    p=softmax(logits)
    best_score=...; best_theta; best_p
    m=np.zeros(K); v=...
    t=0; lam=lam_init; no_improve=0; last_imp=time.monotonic(); last_no? 
    while time.monotonic() < time_end:
       compute p,c,weights,h,score
       if score<best... update last_imp
       # update lam schedule: if no improve > 2s? lam*=1.3 up to max; if >10s at max, restart?
       grad = basis_grad(Phi,p,h)
       adam theta
       logits=np.clip(theta@Phi, -50,50)
    return best_score, best_theta, best_p
```

Basis gradient implementation top-level:
```
def basis_gradient(Phi, p, h):
    # Phi shape K,n, h length n
    ph = p * h
    sum_ph = ph.sum()
    # mean_phi = Phi.dot(p)  length K
    mean_phi = Phi @ p
    # weighted = Phi @ ph
    return weighted - mean_phi * sum_ph
```
This is O(Kn) with BLAS. Use `Phi @ p` and `Phi @ ph`.

Lowfreq noise generation:
```
def lowfreq_noise(n, rng, max_freq=6, std=1.0):
    noise = np.zeros(n)
    i = np.arange(n)
    for k in 1..max_freq:
       amp = rng.uniform(0.5,1.0)/k
       pha = rng.uniform(0,2π)
       noise += amp*np.sin(2π*k*i/n + pha)
    noise -= noise.mean()
    s=noise.std()
    if s>0: noise *= std/s
    return noise
```
This noise has frequencies up to max_freq; good.

Could also random Gaussian smoothed by FFT low-pass, but harmonic fine.

```
    while time.monotonic() < time_end:

         idx = min(len(lam_values)-1, int(frac*len(lam_values)))

         lam = target
         c=conv(p); cmax...
         if score<best_score: update best
         # if no improve for patience_s: restart

         # compute weights, h, g
         # If cmax too high relative? maybe.

         # normalize p
```

Normalization function:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.where(a>0,a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.full_like(a, 1.0/a.size)
    a=a/s
    return a
```
For direct p, after clip, use this.

`project_p_to_theta(Phi, p)`:
```
lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-12))
lp=lp-lp.mean()
# solve Phi.T theta = lp (least squares)
theta, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(Phi.T, lp, rcond=None)
return theta
```

`basis_gradient(Phi, p, h)`:
```
ph = p*h
sum_ph = ph.sum()
mean_phi = Phi.dot(p)
weighted = Phi.dot(ph)
return weighted - mean_phi*sum_ph
```

`optimize_theta_slice(Phi, theta0, start_time, time_end, rng, lam_factors, step_frac_base, patience_s)`:
- n=Phi.shape[1]

- c,cmax,score initial. If score inf, reset theta zero? We'll assume finite.
- m,v length K, t=0, lam = lam_factors[0]*n, lr=step_frac_base, rest=0, last_imp=start_time
- target total = time_end-start_time; if <=1 return current.
- patience = min(patience_s, max(2, 0.25*total))
Loop:
```
while time.monotonic() < time_end:
   now=time.monotonic()
   # if now <= start? 
   c=fast_self_conv(p); cmax=np.max(c); score=2n cmax
   if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
       best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); best_theta=theta.copy(); last_imp=now
   # schedule target factor idx...
   frac=(now-start_time)/total
   idx=min(len(lam)-1, int(frac*len(lam)))
   target_lam=lam_ab[idx]
   lam += 0.1*(target_lam-lam) # plus if restart? Use local variable.
   if now - last_imp > patience*(1+0.25*rest):
       # restart
       scale = 0.5/(1+0.5*rest)
       noise=lowfreq_noise(n,rng, max_freq=6)
       # additive in p? But θ restart: perturb theta
       theta = best_theta + scale * rng.standard_normal(K)
       # maybe project? no
       m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; rest +=1
       lr = max(0.005, lr*0.7)
       lam = lam_ab[0]
       last_imp=now
       p=theta_to_p(Phi,theta)
       continue
   # weights
   x=lam*(c-cmax); x=np.maximum(x, -700.0); w=np.exp(x); s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w uniform; w/=s
   h=corr_weights_p(w,p)
   g=basis_gradient(Phi,p,h)

   t +=1; m=...; theta -= lr*...
   theta=np.clip(theta,-15,15)
   p=theta_to_p(Phi,theta)
```

`resize_sequence` top-level. Use `np.interp`. Need if n_old=1. If arr all zero return uniform. It returns sum1 p.
```
def resize_sequence(a, n_new):
    arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,...)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(n_new)/n_new
    arr=arr/s
    if arr.size==n_new: return arr
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
    y_old=arr / arr.size
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    f_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, y_old)
    p_new=f_new*n_new
    p_new=np.maximum(p_new,0)
    return normalize_nonneg(p_new)
```

`make_initial_shape` randomized; need `style` int. Let's write:
```
def make_initial_shape(n, rng, style=0):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    raw=np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # edge
    add_left = (style % 3) != 2  # style 2 no edge
    al = float(rng.uniform(0.30,0.50))
    n_alpha = n ** al
    if add_left:
       A = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
       if A>1e-3:
          raw += A * n_alpha / (i + 0.5) ** al
    if style % 2 == 0:
       B = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
       if B>1e-3:
          raw += B * n_alpha / (n - i - 0.5) ** al
    # modes
    nf = int(rng.integers(2,8))
    base_amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.6))
    for k in range(1,nf+1):
       amp = base_amp * float(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0)) / float(np.sqrt(k))
       ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
       raw += amp * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*k*i/n + ph)
       # maybe cos for asym
       if rng.random() <0.5:
           amp2= amp*0.5
           ph2=float(rng.uniform(0,2π))
           raw += amp2*np.cos(2π*k*i/n + ph2)
    if style == 5:
       raw *= (1.0 + 0.2*lowfreq_noise(n,rng,8,1.0))
    raw=np.maximum(raw, 1e-3)
    return normalize_nonneg(raw)
```
Using `rng.random()` (float) okay. `rng.integers` returns np int; cast.
Style 2 no left edge? We'll use styles 0..7.

Maybe add a pure antisymmetric shape: `raw = 1 + 0.6*sin(2π*i/n)` plus edge smaller. Random cos/sin covers.

`corr_weights_p` output length n but may have small imaginary discarded. `irfft` returns real length M; slice. Need ensure `M=next_pow2(3*n-2)`. For n=4096, target 12286, M=16384. Good. For n=1, target1. But n large.
Function:
```
def corr_weights_p(w, p):
    n=p.size
    L=2*n-1
    target=3*n-2
    M=next_pow2(target)
    pr=p[::-1]
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(pr, M)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fp, M)
    out=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)
```
For n=8192, M=32768? target=24574, next_pow2? (24574-1).bit_length =15? 2^15=32768 yes. For n=16384 target=49150, next pow2 65536. Good.
Convolution length = len(pr)+len(w)-1 =n+2n-1-1=3n-2. Good.

```
def optimize_basis(n, time_start, time_end, rng, initial_ps):
    Phi=build_basis(n,rng)
    K=Phi.shape[0]
    starts=[]
    for p in initial_ps:
       if p is not None:
          try:
             th=project_p_to_theta(Phi, normalize_nonneg(p))
             if finite: starts.append(th)
          except: pass
    for k in range(12): starts.append(rng.normal(size=K, scale=0.6))
    if not starts: starts.append(np.zeros(K))
    total=time_end-time_start
    first_end=time_start+0.45*total
    results=[]
    # first pass
    i=0
    while i < len(starts) and time.monotonic() < first_end:
        end = min(time.monotonic()+max(1.0, (first_end-time_start)/float(max(1,len(starts)))), first_end)
        sc,th,p = optimize_theta_slice(Phi, starts[i], time.monotonic(), end, rng, [0.5,1.0,3.0,10.0], 0.05, 6.0)
        results.append((sc,p))
        i += 1
    results=sort_scored_list(results)
    top=results[:6]
    # second pass
    j=0
    while j < len(top) and time.monotonic() < time_end:
       p0 = top[j][1]
       th0=project_p_to_theta(Phi, normalize_nonneg(p0))
       end = min(time.monotonic() + max(1.0, (time_end - max(time.monotonic(), first_end))/max(1,len(top))), time_end)
       sc,th,p = optimize_theta_slice(Phi, th0, time.monotonic(), end, rng, [1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0], 0.035, 8.0)
       results.append((sc,p))
       j +=1
    results=sort_scored_list(results)
    out=[]
    for sc,p in results[:5]: out.append(p)
    return out
```

Code skeleton:

```
"""..."""
import numpy as np
import time

def next_pow2(size):
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()

def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=1e-18):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if floor and floor > 0.0:
        a = a + floor
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s

def lowfreq_noise(n, rng, max_freq=6, std=1.0):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, int(max_freq) + 1):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.0)) / float(np.sqrt(k))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        noise += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * i / float(n) + ph)
    noise -= noise.mean()
    sd = float(noise.std())
    if sd > 1e-15:
        noise *= std / sd
    return noise

def fast_self_conv(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    target = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_pow2(target)
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)
    return c[:target]
```

`score_from_conv`:
```
def score_from_conv(p, c):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return np.inf
    return 2.0 * p.size * cmax
```

`corr_weights_p`:
...

`center_scale`:
```
def center_scale(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=a-a.mean()
    sd=float(a.std())
    if sd>1e-12:
        a=a/sd
    return a
```

`build_basis`: use center_scale.

`project_p_to_theta`:
`Phi.T` may be large; if shape n x K, okay.
```
def project_p_to_theta(Phi, p):
    p=normalize_nonneg(p)
    lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-12))
    lp=lp-lp.mean()
    try:
       theta,_ = np.linalg.lstsq(Phi.T, lp, rcond=None)
    except Exception:
       theta=np.zeros(Phi.shape[0])
    return np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```

`theta_to_p`:
```
def theta_to_p(Phi, theta):
    logits=theta.dot(Phi)
    logits=np.nan_to_num(logits, nan=0.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    m=float(logits.max())
    diff=logits - m
    diff=diff.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    diff=np.minimum(diff, 0.0)
    diff=np.maximum(diff, -700.0)
    e=np.exp(diff)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0.0:
        return np.full(Phi.shape[1], 1.0/Phi.shape[1])
    return e/s
```

`basis_gradient`:
...

`sort_scored_list`:
```
def sort_scored_list(results):
    if len(results) <= 1:
        return list(results)
    scores=np.empty(len(results),dtype=np.float64)
    for i,(s,p) in enumerate(results):
        vals=float(s)
        if not np.isfinite(vals): vals=np.inf
        scores[i]=vals
    order=np.argsort(scores)
    out=[]
    for idx in order:
        out.append(results[int(idx)])
    return out
```

`update_best`:
```
def update_best(score, p, best_score, best_p):
    if p is None:
        return best_score, best_p
    vals=float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(vals):
        return best_score, best_p
    arr=np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if best_p is None or vals < best_score:
        return vals, arr
    return best_score, best_p
```
It doesn't copy if not better; okay.

`optimize_theta_slice`:
Let's write in full.
```
def optimize_theta_slice(Phi, theta0, start_time, time_end, rng, lam_factors, step_frac_base, patience_s):
    K = Phi.shape[0]
    n = Phi.shape[1]
    theta = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)
    c = fast_self_conv(p)
    score = score_from_conv(p, c)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        theta = np.zeros(K)
        p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)
        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        score = score_from_conv(p, c)
    best_score = score
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_theta = theta.copy()
    total = max(0.0, float(time_end - start_time))
    if total <= 0.5:
        return best_score, best_theta, best_p
    lam_abs = np.asarray(lam_factors, dtype=np.float64) * float(n)
    lam_abs = np.maximum(lam_abs, 1e-6)
    m = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lam = float(lam_abs[0])
    lr = float(step_frac_base)
    rest = 0
    last_imp = max(start_time, time.monotonic())
    patience = min(float(patience_s), max(2.0, 0.25 * total))
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    while time.monotonic() < time_end:
        now = time.monotonic()
        # Compute current score
        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        score = score_from_conv(p, c)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_theta = theta.copy()
            last_imp = now
        if (not np.isfinite(c.max())) or float(c.max()) <= 0.0:
            p = best_p.copy()
            continue
        frac = (now - start_time) / total
        if frac < 0.0: frac=0.0
        if frac > 1.0: frac=1.0
        idx = int(frac * float(len(lam_abs)))
        if idx >= len(lam_abs): idx = len(lam_abs)-1
        target = float(lam_abs[idx])
        lam += 0.25 * (target - lam)
        if now - last_imp > patience * (1.0 + 0.25 * float(rest)):
            scale = 0.6 / (1.0 + 0.4 * float(rest))
            theta = best_theta + scale * rng.standard_normal(K)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -12.0, 12.0)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            rest += 1
            lr = max(0.005, lr * 0.75)
            lam = float(lam_abs[0])
            last_imp = now
            p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)
            continue
        x = lam * (c - float(np.max(c)))
        x = np.maximum(x, -700.0)
        w = np.exp(x)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size)
        else:
            w = w / ws
        h = corr_weights_p(w, p)
        g = basis_gradient(Phi, p, h)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
        bc1 = 1.0 if t > 100 else (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        bc2 = 1.0 if t > 100 else (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        step = lr * (m / bc1) / (np.sqrt(v / bc2) + eps)
        theta = theta - step
        theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=12.0, neginf=-12.0)
        theta = np.clip(theta, -12.0, 12.0)
        p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)
    return best_score, best_theta, best_p
```
Note: In weights, `float(np.max(c))` recomputed; we already have cmax maybe. We can store cmax from score. But okay. For speed, store `cmax = float(np.max(c))` once after c. Use that. Modify:
```
cmax = float(np.max(c))
score=2n*cmax if finite
...
x = lam*(c-cmax)
```

```
def optimize_p_slice(p0, start_time, time_end, rng, lam_factors, step_frac_base, patience_s):

    m=zeros; ...

       c=fast_self_conv(p); cmax=...
       score=...
       if improve update
       if cmax not finite or <=0: p=best_p.copy(); continue
       frac...

          noise=lowfreq_noise(n,rng, max_freq=4+rest%4)
          noise=noise-noise.mean()

       g = nan_to_num
       t++ Adam; p -= lr*...
       p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0)
       p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
```

`optimize_p_schedule`:
```
def optimize_p_schedule(p0, start_time, time_end, rng):
    total = max(0.0, time_end-start_time)
    if total <= 20.0:
       return optimize_p_slice(p0, start_time, time_end, rng, (2.0,8.0,20.0,40.0), 0.08, 5.0)
    t1 = start_time + 0.55*total
    t2 = start_time + 0.85*total
    score,p = optimize_p_slice(p0, start_time, t1, rng, (0.5,1.0,3.0,8.0), 0.25, 10.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t2:
       score,p = optimize_p_slice(p, time.monotonic(), t2, rng, (8.0,16.0,30.0,50.0), 0.12, 12.0)
    score,p = optimize_p_slice(p, time.monotonic(), time_end, rng, (50.0,80.0,100.0), 0.03, 15.0)
    return score,p
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start + total - 12.0
    best_score = np.inf
    best_p = None
    parent_arr=None
    try:
        if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
            cand = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
            if cand is not None:
                arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if arr.size > 0:
                    parent_arr=arr
    except Exception:
        parent_arr=None
    if parent_arr is not None:
        par=normalize_nonneg(parent_arr)
        c=fast_self_conv(par)
        sc=score_from_conv(par,c)
        best_score,best_p=update_best(sc,par,best_score,best_p)
    avail = max(0.0, deadline - start)
    basis_time = min(250.0, max(60.0, 0.10*avail))
    main_time = min(1200.0, max(600.0, 0.52*avail))
    upgrade_time = max(0.0, avail - basis_time - main_time - 4.0)
    t_basis_end = start + basis_time
    basis_results=[]
    if time.monotonic() < t_basis_end:
        n_basis=4096

    n_main = 8192
    main_start = time.monotonic()
    t_main_end = main_start + min(main_time, max(0.0, t_basis_end + main_time - main_start?))
```

Hard to account second pass reserved. We can just allocate `slice_main = max(20.0, main_total / 7.0)` fixed. If time left more, slices equal. That's fine. Use fixed.

```
for i,p0 in top5:
  if now>=main_end break
  end=min(now+slice_main, main_end)
  if end-now<5: break
  run
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step functions minimizing
    2 n max_k conv(a,a)[k] / (sum a)^2.

The program uses:
  * a small basis-phase optimization in a 4096-point subspace,
  * direct projected log-sum-exp optimization on larger lengths (8192/16384),
  * multi-start with edge-singular and smooth asymmetric initializations,
  * adaptive temperature lambda scaled with n so that exp(lambda*(c-max(c)))
    is a useful flatness surrogate,
  * final verification with the provided evaluator.

No nested helpers are used.  Import evaluate_sequence is assumed to be
available in the global namespace.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(size):
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=1e-18):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if floor is not None and floor > 0.0:
        a = a + floor
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def lowfreq_noise(n, rng, max_freq=6, std=1.0):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    mf = int(max_freq)
    for k in range(1, mf + 1):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.0)) / float(np.sqrt(float(k)))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        noise += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * i / float(n) + ph)
    noise -= float(noise.mean())
    sd = float(noise.std())
    if sd > 1e-15:
        noise *= std / sd
    return noise


def fast_self_conv(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    target = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_pow2(target)
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)
    return c[:target]


def score_from_conv(p, c):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(p.size) * cmax


def corr_weights_p(w, p):
    n = int(p.size)
    target = 3 * n - 2
    M = next_pow2(target)
    pr = p[::-1]
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(pr, M)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp, M)
    out = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def center_scale(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a - float(a.mean())
    sd = float(a.std())
    if sd > 1e-12:
        a = a / sd
    return a


def build_basis(n, rng):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = i / float(n)
    arrs = []

    # Edge singularities with several exponents.
    for a in (0.30, 0.40, 0.45):
        n_pow = float(n) ** a
        left = n_pow / (i + 0.5) ** a
        right = n_pow / (float(n) - i - 0.5) ** a
        arrs.append(center_scale(left))
        arrs.append(center_scale(right))

    # Low-order polynomials.
    for pw in (1, 2, 3):
        a = (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** pw
        arrs.append(center_scale(a))

    # Low-frequency trigonometric modes.
    for k in range(1, 7):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * i / float(n)
        arrs.append(center_scale(np.sin(ang)))
        arrs.append(center_scale(np.cos(ang)))

    # Edge-modulated linear terms.
    left_raw = (float(n) ** 0.45) / (i + 0.5) ** 0.45
    right_raw = (float(n) ** 0.45) / (float(n) - i - 0.5) ** 0.45
    arrs.append(center_scale(left_raw * x))
    arrs.append(center_scale(right_raw * (1.0 - x)))

    Phi = np.vstack(arrs).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    return Phi


def project_p_to_theta(Phi, p):
    p = normalize_nonneg(p)
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-12))
    lp = lp - float(lp.mean())
    try:
        theta, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(Phi.T, lp, rcond=None)
    except Exception:
        theta = np.zeros(Phi.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return theta


def theta_to_p(Phi, theta):
    logits = theta.dot(Phi)
    logits = np.nan_to_num(logits, nan=0.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    m = float(logits.max())
    diff = logits - m
    diff = np.minimum(diff, 0.0)
    diff = np.maximum(diff, -700.0)
    e = np.exp(diff)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(Phi.shape[1], 1.0 / float(Phi.shape[1]), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def basis_gradient(Phi, p, h):
    ph = p * h
    sum_ph = float(ph.sum())
    mean_phi = Phi.dot(p)
    weighted = Phi.dot(ph)
    g = weighted - mean_phi * sum_ph
    return np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def sort_scored_list(results):
    if len(results) <= 1:
        return list(results)
    scores = np.empty(len(results), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(len(results)):
        vals = float(results[i][0])
        if not np.isfinite(vals):
            vals = np.inf
        scores[i] = vals
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    out = []
    for idx in order:
        out.append(results[int(idx)])
    return out


def update_best(score, p, best_score, best_p):
    if p is None:
        return best_score, best_p
    vals = float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(vals):
        return best_score, best_p
    if best_p is None or vals < best_score:
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        return vals, arr
    return best_score, best_p


def optimize_theta_slice(Phi, theta0, start_time, time_end, rng,
                         lam_factors, step_frac_base, patience_s):
    K = int(Phi.shape[0])
    n = int(Phi.shape[1])

    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)

    c = fast_self_conv(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        theta = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)
        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        theta = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)
        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax

    best_score = score
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_theta = theta.copy()

    total = max(0.0, float(time_end - start_time))
    if total <= 0.5:
        return best_score, best_theta, best_p

    lam_abs = np.asarray(lam_factors, dtype=np.float64) * float(n)
    lam_abs = np.maximum(lam_abs, 1e-6)

    m = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lam = float(lam_abs[0])
    lr = float(step_frac_base)
    rest = 0
    last_imp = max(start_time, time.monotonic())
    patience = min(float(patience_s), max(2.0, 0.25 * total))
    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    while time.monotonic() < time_end:
        now = time.monotonic()

        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            p = best_p.copy()
            continue
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_theta = theta.copy()
            last_imp = now

        frac = (now - start_time) / total
        if frac < 0.0:
            frac = 0.0
        if frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0
        idx = int(frac * float(len(lam_abs)))
        if idx >= len(lam_abs):
            idx = len(lam_abs) - 1
        target = float(lam_abs[idx])
        lam += 0.25 * (target - lam)

        if now - last_imp > patience * (1.0 + 0.25 * float(rest)):
            scale = 0.6 / (1.0 + 0.4 * float(rest))
            theta = best_theta + scale * rng.standard_normal(K)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -12.0, 12.0)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            rest += 1
            lr = max(0.005, lr * 0.75)
            lam = float(lam_abs[0])
            last_imp = now
            p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)
            continue

        x = lam * (c - cmax)
        x = np.maximum(x, -700.0)
        w = np.exp(x)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / ws

        h = corr_weights_p(w, p)
        g = basis_gradient(Phi, p, h)

        t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
        bc1 = 1.0 if t > 100 else (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        bc2 = 1.0 if t > 100 else (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        step = lr * (m / bc1) / (np.sqrt(v / bc2) + eps)
        theta = theta - step
        theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=12.0, neginf=-12.0)
        theta = np.clip(theta, -12.0, 12.0)
        p = theta_to_p(Phi, theta)

    return best_score, best_theta, best_p


def optimize_basis(n, time_start, time_end, rng, initial_ps):
    Phi = build_basis(n, rng)
    K = int(Phi.shape[0])

    starts = []
    for p in initial_ps:
        if p is None:
            continue
        try:
            th = project_p_to_theta(Phi, normalize_nonneg(p))
            if np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
                starts.append(th)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Random structured starts.
    for _ in range(12):
        starts.append(rng.normal(size=K, scale=0.6))

    if not starts:
        starts.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))

    total = max(0.0, float(time_end - time_start))
    if total <= 2.0:
        out = []
        for p in initial_ps[:3]:
            if p is not None:
                pp = normalize_nonneg(p)
                out.append(pp)
        return out

    first_end = time_start + 0.45 * total
    results = []

    i = 0
    while i < len(starts) and time.monotonic() < first_end:
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = first_end - now
        if remaining < 1.5:
            break
        slice_len = max(2.0, remaining / float(max(1, len(starts) - i)))
        end = min(now + slice_len, first_end)
        sc, th, p = optimize_theta_slice(
            Phi, starts[i], now, end, rng,
            (0.5, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0), 0.05, 6.0)
        results.append((sc, p))
        i += 1

    results = sort_scored_list(results)
    top = results[:6]

    j = 0
    while j < len(top) and time.monotonic() < time_end:
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = time_end - now
        if remaining < 1.5:
            break
        slice_len = max(2.0, remaining / float(max(1, len(top) - j)))
        end = min(now + slice_len, time_end)
        p0 = normalize_nonneg(top[j][1])
        th0 = project_p_to_theta(Phi, p0)
        sc, th, p = optimize_theta_slice(
            Phi, th0, now, end, rng,
            (1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0), 0.035, 8.0)
        results.append((sc, p))
        j += 1

    results = sort_scored_list(results)
    out = []
    for sc, p in results[:5]:
        pp = normalize_nonneg(p)
        out.append(pp)
    return out


def optimize_p_slice(p0, start_time, time_end, rng,
                     lam_factors, step_frac_base, patience_s):
    n = int(p0.size)
    p = normalize_nonneg(p0)

    c = fast_self_conv(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax

    best_score = score
    best_p = p.copy()

    total = max(0.0, float(time_end - start_time))
    if total <= 0.5:
        return best_score, best_p

    lam_abs = np.asarray(lam_factors, dtype=np.float64) * float(n)
    lam_abs = np.maximum(lam_abs, 1e-6)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    lam = float(lam_abs[0])
    lr = float(step_frac_base) / float(n)
    min_lr = 0.01 / float(n)
    rest = 0
    last_imp = max(start_time, time.monotonic())
    patience = min(float(patience_s), max(2.0, 0.20 * total))

    b1 = 0.9
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    while time.monotonic() < time_end:
        now = time.monotonic()

        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            p = best_p.copy()
            continue
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            last_imp = now

        frac = (now - start_time) / total
        if frac < 0.0:
            frac = 0.0
        if frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0
        idx = int(frac * float(len(lam_abs)))
        if idx >= len(lam_abs):
            idx = len(lam_abs) - 1
        target = float(lam_abs[idx])
        lam += 0.25 * (target - lam)

        if now - last_imp > patience * (1.0 + 0.25 * float(rest)):
            noise = lowfreq_noise(n, rng, max_freq=4 + (rest % 4), std=1.0)
            noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
            scale = 1.0 + 2.0 * float(rng.random())
            p = best_p * (1.0 + 0.25 * noise)
            p = p + scale * (1.0 / float(n)) * noise
            p = normalize_nonneg(p)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            rest += 1
            lr = max(min_lr, lr * 0.75)
            lam = float(lam_abs[0])
            last_imp = now
            continue

        x = lam * (c - cmax)
        x = np.maximum(x, -700.0)
        w = np.exp(x)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / ws

        h = corr_weights_p(w, p)
        g = h - float(h.mean())
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
        bc1 = 1.0 if t > 100 else (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        bc2 = 1.0 if t > 100 else (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        step = lr * (m / bc1) / (np.sqrt(v / bc2) + eps)
        p = p - step
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        p = normalize_nonneg(p)

    return best_score, best_p


def optimize_p_schedule(p0, start_time, time_end, rng):
    total = max(0.0, float(time_end - start_time))
    if total <= 25.0:
        return optimize_p_slice(p0, start_time, time_end, rng,
                                (2.0, 8.0, 20.0, 40.0), 0.08, 5.0)

    t1 = start_time + 0.55 * total
    t2 = start_time + 0.85 * total

    sc, p = optimize_p_slice(p0, start_time, t1, rng,
                             (0.5, 1.0, 3.0, 8.0), 0.22, 10.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t2:
        sc, p = optimize_p_slice(p, time.monotonic(), t2, rng,
                                 (8.0, 16.0, 30.0, 50.0), 0.11, 12.0)
    sc, p = optimize_p_slice(p, time.monotonic(), time_end, rng,
                             (50.0, 80.0, 100.0), 0.03, 15.0)
    return sc, p


def make_initial_shape(n, rng, style=0):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    raw = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Edge boosts, which are needed to leave the uniform stationary point.
    if (style % 3) != 2:
        al = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.50))
        n_alpha = float(n) ** al
        A = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
        if A > 1e-3:
            raw += A * n_alpha / (i + 0.5) ** al
    if (style % 2) == 0:
        if "al" not in locals():
            al = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.50))
            n_alpha = float(n) ** al
        B = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
        if B > 1e-3:
            raw += B * n_alpha / (float(n) - i - 0.5) ** al

    # Smooth asymmetric Fourier content.
    nf = int(rng.integers(2, 8))
    base_amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45))
    for k in range(1, nf + 1):
        amp = base_amp * float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)) / float(np.sqrt(float(k)))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        raw += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * i / float(n) + ph)
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
            amp2 = 0.5 * amp
            ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            raw += amp2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * i / float(n) + ph2)

    if style == 7:
        raw *= (1.0 + 0.2 * lowfreq_noise(n, rng, max_freq=8, std=1.0))

    raw = np.maximum(raw, 1e-3)
    return normalize_nonneg(raw)


def resize_sequence(a, n_new):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n_new, 1.0 / float(n_new), dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size == int(n_new):
        return arr
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    y_old = arr / float(arr.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    f_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, y_old)
    p_new = f_new * float(n_new)
    p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0.0)
    return normalize_nonneg(p_new)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start + total - 15.0

    best_score = np.inf
    best_p = None

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
            cand = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
            if cand is not None:
                arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if arr.size > 0:
                    parent_arr = arr
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None:
        par = normalize_nonneg(parent_arr)
        c = fast_self_conv(par)
        sc = score_from_conv(par, c)
        best_score, best_p = update_best(sc, par, best_score, best_p)
        print("parent internal score = %.7f len=%d" % (best_score, par.size))

    avail = max(0.0, deadline - start)
    basis_time = min(240.0, max(60.0, 0.10 * avail))
    main_time = min(1200.0, max(600.0, 0.52 * avail))
    upgrade_time = max(0.0, avail - basis_time - main_time - 5.0)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: low-dimensional basis search at n=4096
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    t_basis_end = start + basis_time
    if time.monotonic() < t_basis_end:
        n_basis = 4096
        initial_basis = []
        if parent_arr is not None:
            pp = resize_sequence(parent_arr, n_basis)
            if pp is not None:
                initial_basis.append(pp)
        for k in range(12):
            initial_basis.append(make_initial_shape(n_basis, rng, k))

        basis_results = optimize_basis(n_basis, time.monotonic(), t_basis_end,
                                       rng, initial_basis)
        for p in basis_results:
            c = fast_self_conv(p)
            sc = score_from_conv(p, c)
            best_score, best_p = update_best(sc, p, best_score, best_p)
        print("after basis phase: best internal score = %.7f len=%s" %
              (best_score, best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0))

    basis_actual_end = time.monotonic()

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: full search at n=8192
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    n_main = 8192
    main_end = min(deadline, base_actual_end if False else max(0.0, basis_actual_end + main_time))
    main_end = min(deadline, max(basis_actual_end, main_end))
    if main_end <= time.monotonic():
        main_end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + min(60.0, avail * 0.1))

    starts = []
    for p in basis_results[:4]:
        pp = resize_sequence(p, n_main)
        if pp is not None:
            starts.append(pp)

    if parent_arr is not None:
        pp = resize_sequence(parent_arr, n_main)
        if pp is not None:
            starts.append(pp)

    if best_p is not None and int(best_p.size) != n_main:
        pp = resize_sequence(best_p, n_main)
        if pp is not None:
            starts.append(pp)

    for k in range(8):
        starts.append(make_initial_shape(n_main, rng, 10 + k))

    if not starts:
        starts.append(np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_main))

    scored = []
    for p in starts:
        pp = normalize_nonneg(p)
        c = fast_self_conv(pp)
        sc = score_from_conv(pp, c)
        scored.append((sc, pp))
    scored = sort_scored_list(scored)
    if len(scored) > 0:
        print("best 8192 start internal score = %.7f" % scored[0][0])

    main_total = max(0.0, main_end - time.monotonic())
    if main_total > 10.0:
        top_count = 5
        second_count = 2
        slice_main = main_total / float(max(1, top_count + second_count + 1))
        slice_main = max(20.0, slice_main)

        pass1 = []
        top5 = scored[:top_count]
        for i in range(len(top5)):
            if time.monotonic() >= main_end:
                break
            now = time.monotonic()
            end = min(now + slice_main, main_end)
            if end - now < 5.0:
                break
            sc0, p0 = top5[i]
            sc, p = optimize_p_schedule(p0, now, end, rng)
            pass1.append((sc, p))
            best_score, best_p = update_best(sc, p, best_score, best_p)

        if len(pass1) > 0:
            pass1 = sort_scored_list(pass1)
            top2 = pass1[:second_count]
        else:
            top2 = scored[:second_count]

        for i in range(len(top2)):
            if time.monotonic() >= main_end:
                break
            now = time.monotonic()
            end = min(now + slice_main, main_end)
            if end - now < 5.0:
                break
            sc0, p0 = top2[i]
            sc, p = optimize_p_schedule(p0, now, end, rng)
            pass1.append((sc, p))
            best_score, best_p = update_best(sc, p, best_score, best_p)

        if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < main_end:
            sc, p = optimize_p_slice(normalize_nonneg(best_p),
                                     time.monotonic(), main_end, rng,
                                     (50.0, 80.0, 100.0), 0.025, 20.0)
            best_score, best_p = update_best(sc, p, best_score, best_p)

        print("after 8192 phase: best internal score = %.7f len=%s" %
              (best_score, best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0))

    main_actual_end = time.monotonic()

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: optional upgrade to n=16384
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - main_actual_end)
    up_time = min(upgrade_time, remaining)
    if best_p is not None and up_time > 60.0 and int(best_p.size) < 16384:
        n_up = 16384
        up_starts = []
        pp = resize_sequence(best_p, n_up)
        if pp is not None:
            up_starts.append(pp)

        noise = lowfreq_noise(n_up, rng, max_freq=12, std=1.0)
        pp2 = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64) +
                               (0.5 / float(n_up)) * noise)
        up_starts.append(pp2)

        up_total = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        slice_up = max(30.0, up_total / 3.0)

        for p0 in up_starts:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            now = time.monotonic()
            end = min(now + slice_up, deadline)
            if end - now < 5.0:
                break
            sc, p = optimize_p_schedule(p0, now, end, rng)
            best_score, best_p = update_best(sc, p, best_score, best_p)

        if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            sc, p = optimize_p_slice(normalize_nonneg(best_p),
                                     time.monotonic(), deadline, rng,
                                     (60.0, 80.0, 100.0), 0.02, 20.0)
            best_score, best_p = update_best(sc, p, best_score, best_p)

        print("after upgrade phase: best internal score = %.7f len=%s" %
              (best_score, best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0))

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0

    # Final normalization and safety clipping.
    out_arr = normalize_nonneg(best_p)
    out = []
    for val in out_arr:
        vv = float(val)
        if vv < 0.0:
            vv = 0.0
        if vv > 1000.0:
            vv = 1000.0
        out.append(vv)

    verified = np.inf
    if time.monotonic() < start + total - 4.0:
        try:
            verified = evaluate_sequence(out)
        except Exception:
            verified = np.inf

    print("final internal score = %.7f, verified = %.7f, len = %d, elapsed = %.1fs" %
          (best_score, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - start))
    return out
```
